EMPLOYEES’ STATE INSURANCE CORPORATION 

New Delhi, the 16th December 1987 

R-12/19/9/84-Ins.I.—In supersession to Notification of 
even No. dated 23rd November, 1984, 18th December, 1986 
and 24th February, 1987, the Director General, Employees’ 
State Insurance Corporation, in exercise of the powers under 
Regulation 75 of the ESI (General) Regulations, 1950, read 
with the Resolution of the Employees’ State Insurance Cor¬ 
poration dated 14th December, 1980, has re-constituted the 
Peripatetic Medical Board for the purpose of Section 54 and 
54-A of the ESI Act, .1948, as under:— 

Chairman 

1. Dr. S. P. Bhatnagar, 

Dy. Medical Commissioner, 

Delhi. 

Alternate Chairman in the absence of 
Dr. S. P. Bhatnagar; 

Dr. (Mrs.) Helen Singh, 

Dy. Medical Commissioner, 

Family Welfare, 

ESIC hospital Complex, 

Basaidarapur, New Delhi. 

Members 

2. Dr. N. D. Khurana, 

D. Orthopaedics, 

I.M.O. Grade-I, 

ESI Hospital, Basaidarapur, New Delhi. 

3. Dr. S. R. Chauhan, M.S., 

Surgical Specialist, ESI Hospital. 

Basaidarpur, New Delhi. 

The Chairman has been authorised to co-opt any other 
Specialist as a member of the Board depending upon the ail¬ 
ment from which insured person to be examined is suffering, 
in lieu of or in addition to the Orthopaedic and Surgical 
Specialist from any ESI Hospital, if necessary. 

Jurisdiction :— 

The Board shall have jurisdiction over all areas throughout 
India including all areas for which Medical Boards have 
been set up by the ESI Corporation or by different State 
Govts./Union Territories Administration. 


N. VYAS, 
Insurance Commissioneer.

NCR PLANNING BOARD 
. New Delhi, the 14th December 1987 

No. C-11031/1 /86-NCRPB.—In exercise of the powers 
conferred by section 37' of the National Capital Region Plan¬ 
ning Board Act, 1985 (2 of 1985), the Board with the pre¬ 
vious approval of the Central Government, hereby makes 
the following amendment in the National Capital Region 
Planning Board Regulations, 1986, namely :— 

1. (1) These regulations may be calldd, the National Capi¬ 

tal Region Planning Board (Amendment) Regula¬ 
tions, 1987. 

(2) They shall come into force from the date of their 
publication in the official Gazette. 

2. In the National Capital Region Planning Board Regula¬ 
tions, 1986, for regulation 5, the following regulation shall be 
substituted, namely :— 

“5. House Rent Allowance : 


[Part III—Sec. 4 


to 20% (twenty per cent) of their pre-revised pay 
as House Rent Allowance. 

(2) The officers and employees of the Board who haye 
opted for the revised scales of pay as recommended 
by the Fourth Pay Commission shall be entitled to 
House Rent Allowance at the slab rates as pres¬ 
cribed by the Government on the recommendation 
of the Fourth Pay Commission with effect from the 
1st October, 1986. 

(3) Other conditions for drawal of House Rent Allow¬ 
ance for all categories of employees shall be the 
same as are applicable to the Central Government 
employees”.
